NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Friday, November 16, 2018 *SPECIAL MEETING
ONE OR MORE SUPERVISORS MAY ATTEND VIA VIDEO AND/OR TELEPHONICALLY 
The Navajo County Board of Supervisors will hold a special meeting open to the public on Friday, November 16, 2018, in the
Supervisors’ Chamber in the Navajo County Governmental Complex, Holbrook, Arizona. The Board may vote to meet in a closed
executive session to discuss certain matters as indicated on the following agenda. The Board may meet in executive session
pursuant to A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (3) for legal advice on any item.
Persons with disabilities who need accommodation to attend or participate in the meeting may contact the Clerk of the Board’s office
at (928) 524-4053; at least 48 hours prior to the meeting so an accommodation can be arranged.
Items on the agenda may be considered out of order at the Chairman’s discretion. A copy of the agenda background material
provided to the Board, with the exception of items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection at Navajo
County website, www.navajocountyaz.gov 
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: 
           
1. ELECTIONS:  
 
0. Consideration of approval of the Official Canvass of the November 6, 2018 General Election.
 
 
Presented By: Rayleen Richards
 
2. ADJOURN  
 
Dated: November 14, 2018 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
Posted: 3:45 p.m. By: MB                         
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisors Special 1.0.        
Meeting Date: 11/16/2018  
Title: 11-16 Canvass Nov. 6, 2018 Election
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Presented By: Rayleen Richards
Motion before the Board:
Consideration of approval of the Official Canvass of the November 6, 2018 General Election.
 
Background:
Pursuant to A.R.S. § 16-642
 
Canvass of election; postponements
  
The governing body holding an election shall meet and canvass the election not less than
six days nor more than twenty days following the election.
1.
Recommendation: 
office recommends approval
